economic activities, which in turn led to both sales revenue and profit   2020 年 總 收 入 比 去 年 下 跌 4.7% 至 258.7

recent years, as well as the restriction of new capacity investment       保政策趨嚴，新增產能投放受限，行業供
owing to the tightening of environmental protection policies, both        需開始偏緊。儘管去年初中美簽署首階段
supply and demand within the industry have remained low. In the           貿易協議，國際貿易的不確定性曾有所緩
international arena, the signing of the first phase of the US-China       和，但隨後出現新型冠狀病毒疫情，加之
trade agreement at the beginning of last year has reduced                 中美貿易摩擦仍在持續，消費者的消費信
uncertainties. However, the COVID-19 pandemic and the ongoing             心備受影響，在造紙成本攀升的壓力下，
tensions in the US-China relationship has affected consumer               行業經營仍然面對挑戰。本集團因應市場
confidence. With the increase in the cost of paper production, the        變化，積極應對。本集團一直致力於通過
operation of the industry is still facing challenges. The Group has       積極整合上游資源，發展涵蓋製漿及廢紙
proactively responded to these changes in the market. We have been        回收的垂直業務模式，向產業鏈一體化方
committed to developing a vertical business model through active          向發展，確保原材料的供應。同時，本集
consolidation of our upstream resources, covering pulp                    團在現有業務基礎上，不斷擴充規模，控

BUSINESS PROSPECTS                                                        業務前景

The COVID-19 pandemic is under control in China and the domestic          新型冠狀病毒疫情在中國已受控，國內
market is demonstrating a recovery in demand. In 2020, the GDP            市 場 需 求 有 所 復 甦 ， 2020 年 國 內 生 產 總
exceeded 100 trillion yuan for the first time. The Group will make a      值更首次突破100萬億元人民幣。本集團
proactive effort to optimize the industrial chain in order to diversify   積極優化產業鏈，以實現紙張和紙漿業務
its paper and pulp businesses. In the meantime, leveraging the            的多元化。同時，在原有生產規模的基礎
foundation of its existing business, the Group will support the “Belt     上，本集團響應「一帶一路」戰略，瞄準東
& Road” initiatives, targeting the Southeast Asia market for              南亞市場，拓展國際產業布局，並分別在
international development, and introduce new production capacities        越南、馬來西亞、印尼等東南亞國家增加
in countries such as Vietnam, Malaysia and Indonesia. In the future,      新產能，未來將繼續在一帶一路國家以及
we will continue to seek business opportunities in countries and cities   城市發掘商機，本集團對於一帶一路造紙
along the Belt & Road and are confident in the long-term                  業的長遠發展抱有信心。
development of the region’s paper industry.


In regard to the tissue paper business, the Group considers it a major    衛生紙業務方面，本集團高度重視生活用
focus and has therefore established an integrated product chain. Lee      紙業務，已經實現全產品鏈打造。理文造
& Man Paper has endeavored to improve the quality of consumer             紙一直致力於提高生活用紙的質量和生產
tissue and boost its scale of production. The Group produces natural      規模，所生產之天然竹纖維原色衛生紙，
bamboo unbleached tissue in order to satisfy people’s needs for           能滿足消費者對健康和環保的要求，以及
health and environmental protection as well as consumption                消費需求。本集團衛生紙業務總體發展平
requirements. The tissue business has maintained steady growth as a       穩，現時總年產能已達90萬噸，將以市場
whole and our existing capacity reached up to 900,000 tons annually.      及客戶需求為導向，繼續採取有效的策略
The Group is focusing on the requirements of consumers and                來發展衛生紙業務。
continues to adopt effective strategies for developing the tissue
business.


Packaging paper remains the core business of the Group. With the          包裝紙業務是本集團的主要核心業務。隨
increasing prevalence of online consumption in Mainland China and         著內地和海外的網購越來越盛行，市場對
overseas, there is growing demand for quality packaging paper in the      高質量包裝紙需求亦日益增加，加上內地
market. This coupled with the implementation of plastic limit order in    推行限塑令，有利本集團包裝紙業務。為
Mainland China are favorable to the Group’s packaging paper               配 合 國 內 2021 年 起 全 面 禁 止 進 口 廢 紙 ，
business. To cope with the full import ban on waste paper in              本集團將積極在東南亞地區拓展再生紙漿
Mainland China, effective from 2021, the Group will actively develop      業務以替代進口廢紙，增強原材料配置，
the recycled pulp business in Southeast Asia to replace imported          確保生產成本優勢。
waste paper and strengthen the allocation of raw materials in order
to ensure its production cost advantage.

While providing quality products at reasonable prices to maintain its     本集團致力以優質產品及合理價格取勝，
competitiveness, the Group is also committed to fulfilling its            同時亦盡力履行企業社會責任，透過廢紙
corporate social responsibility. It has reduced pollution and carbon      回收及先進生產技術減低污染和碳排放，
emission by recycling waste paper and adopting advanced production        為客戶帶來更多具環保元素的產品。長遠
technology so as to bring more eco-friendly products to customers.        而言，造紙行業仍然有比較大的發展空
In the long term, the paper manufacturing industry will still have        間。本集團將抓住機遇，拓展市場規模，
greater room for development. The Group will capture the                  保持本集團在造紙行業的市場競爭力。
opportunities to expand its market scale and maintain the Group’s
competitiveness in the paper manufacturing industry.


CONCLUSION                                                                結語

The Group would like to express its sincere gratitude to its employees    本集團衷心感謝一直以來全體員工及股東
and shareholders for their continued support. Leveraging its leading      的大力支持，憑藉本集團於造紙業的龍頭
position in the paper manufacturing industry and its consistently solid   地位以及貫徹始終的穩健發展優勢，進一

BOARD DIVERSITY                                                            董事會多元化

The Company has devised a policy on Board diversity (the “Policy”)         本公司已制定董事會成員多元化政策（「政
to ensure adequate diversity in its Board, with effect from 1 January      策 」）以 確 保 董 事 會 充 分 多 元 化 ， 自 2019
2019.                                                                      年1月1日生效。


Vision                                                                     願景

The Company believes diversity is important to enhance the Board’s         本公司深信，多元化政策能夠鼓勵不同觀
effectiveness by encouraging a diversity of perspectives and to            點百花齊放，對提升董事會效能及維持高
maintain high standards of corporate governance. The Company will          水平企業管治至關重要。本公司將繼續因
continue to monitor and develop new objectives for implementing            應本公司和市場不時的具體需求，在其認
and achieving improved diversity on the Board as and when it               為有需要時監察及訂立新目標，藉以提升
considers appropriate with regard to the specific needs of the             董事會的多元性。
Company and the market from time to time.


Policy Statement                                                           政策聲明

The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a               本公司認可到並接納董事會多元化的成
diversified Board and sees increasing diversity at the Board level as an   效，並將董事會層面的多元化視為保持本
essential element in maintaining the Company’s competitive                 公司競爭優勢的一個重要因素。
advantage.


Measurable Objectives                                                      可計量目標

Selection of candidates will be based on a range of diversity              甄選人選將按一系列多元化範疇為基準，
perspectives, which will include but not limited to gender, age,           當中將包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化及
cultural and educational background, professional experience, skills,      教育背景、專業經驗、技能、知識等。最
knowledge and the like. The ultimate decision will be based on             終將按入選候選人可為本公司及董事會帶
overall contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the        來的整體貢獻而定。
Board and the Company.


Review of the Policy                                                       政策檢討

The Nomination Committee will monitor the implementation of the            提名委員會將監察該政策的執行，並將不
Policy and will from time to time review the Policy, as appropriate, to    時在適當時候檢討該政策，以確保該政策
ensure the effectiveness of the Policy. The Nomination Committee           行之有效。提名委員會將會討論任何或需
will discuss any revisions that may be required, and recommend any         作出的修訂，供董事會考慮及審批。
such revisions to the Board for consideration and approval.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                      風險管理及內部監控

reporting mechanism to facilitate the Group to manage it risks across     制，有效地管理本集團各業務範疇的風
business operations effectively.                                          險。

The Group has established a risk management framework, which               本集團已設立風險管理組織架構，由董事
consists of the Board, the Audit Committee and the Risk                    會 、 審 核 委 員 會 及 風 險 管 理 小 組（ 即 高 級
Management Taskforce (i.e. Senior Management). The Board                   管理層）組成。董事會負責評估及釐定本
determines the nature and extent of risks that shall be taken in           集團達成策略目標時所願意接納的風險性
achieving the Group’s strategic objectives, and has the overall            質及程度，並全權負責監督風險管理及內
responsibility for monitoring the design, implementation and the           部監控系統的設計、實施及監察其整體有
overall effectiveness of risk management and internal control              效性。
systems.


The Group has formulated and adopted Risk Management Policy in             本集團亦已制訂及採納企業風險管理制
providing direction in identifying, evaluating and managing                度，提供有效的識別、評估及管理重大風
significant risks. At least on an annual basis, the Risk Management        險的政策。風險管理小組至少每年一次對
Taskforce identifies risks that would adversely affect the achievement     影響本集團實現業務目標的風險事項進行
of the Group’s objectives, and assesses and prioritises the identified     識別，並通過規範的機制進行評估及排
risks according to a set of standard criteria. Risk mitigation plans and   序，對主要風險制定風險緩解計劃及指定
risk owners are then established and assigned for those risks              風險負責人。
considered to be significant.


In addition, the Group has engaged an independent professional             此外，本集團外聘獨立專業機構提供內部
advisor to provide internal audit services, which assist the Board and     審計服務，以協助董事會及審核委員會

BOARD STATEMENT                                                            董事會聲明

Looking back to 2020, the novel coronavirus disease (COVID-19)             回 首 2020 年 ， 新 冠 肺 炎 疫 情 席 捲 全 球 ，
pandemic swept across the world. Although the paper manufacturing          造紙業亦持續面對來自國際間貿易摩擦及
industry continued to face challenges of international trade frictions,    國內供給側改革及環保法規收緊等因素的
domestic supply-side reform and tightened environmental regulations        挑戰，但本集團仍堅定可持續發展步伐，
and so forth, the Group still strove to achieve sustainable strategic      迎難以上，為達成可持續戰略目標而不懈
goals in line with sustainable development and was able to overcome        努力。
challenges.


The Board assumes the full responsibility of sustainable development,      董事會承諾為本集團的可持續發展事務肩
including formulating the long-term, medium-term and short-term            負全責，包括制定長、中、短期的發展方
development directions, evaluating the risks and opportunities of the      向、評估本集團重要ESG議題的風險及機
Group’s key ESG issues, reviewing and ensuring the effectiveness of        遇、檢討及確保相關風險管理及內部控制
the relevant risk management and internal control systems. Besides,        系統的有效性。此外，董事會亦督導本集
the Board also supervises senior management of the Group to                團的高級管理層持續優化管治架構，推動
continually optimize the governance structure, promotes the                ESG 措 施 的 落 實 ， 並 定 期 接 受 ESG 專 責 工
implementation of ESG initiatives and reviews reports prepared by          作小組的匯報等等，帶領本集團在可持續
the dedicated working team on a regular basis with an aim of               發展事務上繼續向前邁進。
making progress on sustainable development of the Group.


During the Year, we proactively carried out sustainable development,       在年內我們積極開展可持續發展工作，包
including helping frontline workers to combat the pandemic and             括在助力前線工作者抗擊疫情，做好全年
adopting the pandemic prevention and control measures throughout           疫 情 防 控 工 作 ， 守 護 員 工 健 康 。 在 2020
the year to safeguard the health of employees. After Spring Festival       年春節長假期後，為避免長期不復工復產
holiday, in order to avoid the shortage of daily necessities that may      可能造成的生活物資短缺風險，我們嚴格
be caused by prolonged suspension of work and operation, we have           按照當地政府的部署，在做好疫情防控的
made every effort to resume production by strictly complying with          前提下全力以赴地恢復生產，保障防疫、
local governments’ policies on pandemic control in order to ensure         民生用紙的供應。
the provision of household paper while safeguarding the epidemic
prevention.

the People’s Republic of China” and other applicable local labour          本 集 團 內 部 設 立《 未 成 年 工 使 用 及 禁 止 使

protection and to clarify responsibilities of enterprises and related     響。國家亦鼓勵企業採用先進及最新技術
responsible officers, hoping to create commercial value for all           處理污染物，推動環保發展。

ensure that our green production model of converting waste paper          保產品的綠色生產模式不受影響。
into environmentally-friendly products is not affected.

Setting Up Environmental Team
成立環保小組

Led by our Chief Executive Officer, the environmental team comprises members having the relevant expertise,
including senior flue gas treatment experts, sewage treatment technicians, environmental monitoring professionals
and environmental protection technicians so as to ensure effective implementation of the following measures.
由首席執行官親自帶領，由合資格專才組成，包括高級煙氣治理師、污水處理人員、環境監測人員、環境保護專業
技術人員等，保證下列相關環保措施得以有效執行。

Duties of Environmental Team
環保小組職責

1.    Holding internal control meetings regularly to review environmental issues in the plants, to update themselves
      with applicable environmental laws and regulations and to give advice on any improvements;
2.    On an ongoing basis, the team engages themselves in environmental protection measures to be taken in
      production processes, including those employed to lower water consumption, treat sewage water and air
      emissions more efficiently, save energy and monitoring emissions to ensure continued compliance with high
      environmental protection standards.
1.    定期召開內部監控會議，旨在審閱有關廠房內的環保事宜，通知各成員環保法律及規定的更新條文，並為存
      在改善空間的環節提供適當建議；
2.    不斷完善生產過程中的環保措施，包括減少耗水量、應用更有效的廢水處理技術、節省能源耗用量、採用更
      環保的廢氣處理技術及排放監控等，確保可持續地達到高環保標準。

of coal. As the combustion temperature in this kind of boilers is lower   爐燃燒溫度低於煤粉爐，產生的氮氧化物
than that in pulverised coal boilers, less nitrogen oxides would be       排放較少。
generated.

Cope with Climate Change                                                 氣候變化應對
Global warming is notably affecting the natural climate by increasing    全球變暖顯著影響自然氣候，使得極端天
the frequency and intensity of extreme weather and natural disasters     氣加劇，暴雨、洪水及乾旱等天災的頻
such as rainstorm, flood and drought, which greatly aggravated the       率和強度增加，大幅加劇了各類天災的破
damage and impact of various natural disasters. To cope with the         壞及影響程度。為應對越趨嚴重的氣候威
intensified threat of climate change, management of our Group has        脅，本集團的管理層定期評估生產經營中
regularly assessed the potential risks that may arise from cause         因氣候變化而可能導致的潛在風險，並採
climate change during production and operation and adopted               取預防及應變措施。
preventive and contingency measures.

the “Extreme Weather Catastrophes Contingency Plan“. We classify         氣 災 害 應 急 預 案 》， 按 照 風 險 可 能 造 成 的
the extreme weather catastrophes into four degrees, namely very          危害程度，將極端天氣災害分為四個等
significant, significant, major and general according to the degree of   級：特別重大、重大、較大、一般。同
harm that may be caused by such risks. Meanwhile, we have                時，我們設立了清晰的應變體系，成立了
established a clear contingency system and set up the Steering           極端天氣應急救援領導小組，明文規定了
Committee on Extreme Weather Contingency and Rescue, which               各成員職能、預警行動和應急措施。我們
expressly stipulates functions of each member, alert actions and         亦會日常定期向員工進行宣傳、培訓和演
contingency measures. We arrange publicity, trainings and drills for     習，提高員工的警惕和應災能力，於颱
employees regularly, aiming at enhancing the vigilance and abilities     風、暴雨、雷電、冰雹、洪水等極端天氣
towards disasters. We will assess how bad we will be affected by the     出現時評估影響，做好相關安全措施並提
extreme weather such as hurricane, rainstorm, thunder, hail and          供安全場所讓員工暫避，保障員工生命安
flood so that we can take relevant safety measures and provide a safe    全並避免生產機械損失。同時，各廠房均
place for our employees. With these measures, we will safeguard our      設有熱電站和後備電源，確保廠房持續穩
employees’ safety and avoid losses arising from the malfunction of       定的生產運作，降低因區域性停電而停產

     The Company has applied the Amendments to HKAS 1 and                     本集團於本年度首次應用香港會計
     HKAS 8 for the first time in the current year. The amendments            準則第1號及香港會計準則第8號（修
     provide a new definition of material that states “information is         訂 本 ）。 修 訂 本 為 重 大 提 供 新 的 定
     material if omitting, misstating or obscuring it could reasonably        義，列明「倘遺漏、錯誤陳述或隱瞞
     be expected to influence decisions that the primary users of             資訊可以合理預期會影響一般用途
     general purpose financial statements make on the basis of                財務報表的主要使用者基於該等提
     those financial statements, which provide financial information          供有關特定報告實體之財務資訊的
     about a specific reporting entity.” The amendments also clarify          財務報表作出之決策，則該資訊屬
     that materiality depends on the nature or magnitude of                   重大」。修訂本並釐清在整體財務報
     information, either individually or in combination with other            表的範圍內，重要性取決於資訊的
     information, in the context of the financial statements taken as         性質或幅度（單獨或與其他資訊結合
     a whole.                                                                 使用）。


     The application of the amendments in the current year had no             本年度應用修訂本對綜合財務報表
     impact on the consolidated financial statements.                         並無影響。

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                             綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公

     of preparation of the consolidated financial statements,                                就編製綜合財務報表而言，倘資訊
     information is considered material if such information is                               可以合理預期會影響主要使用者作
     reasonably expected to influence decisions made by primary                              出決策，則此等資訊被認為重大。
     users. In addition, the consolidated financial statements include                       此外，綜合財務報表包括聯交所證
     applicable disclosures required by the Rules Governing the                              券 上 市 規 則（「 上 市 規 則 」）及 香 港 公
     Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the “Listing Rules”)                       司條例規定之適用披露資料。
     and the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                         3.   主要會計政策（續）
      (CONTINUED)

      The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on               綜合財務報表按歷史成本法編製，
      the historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments        惟於每個報告期末按公允值計量之
      that are measured at fair values at the end of each reporting             若干金融工具除外，會計政策闡述
      period, as explained in the accounting policies set out below.            於下文。


      Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the               歷史成本一般按照交換貨物及服務
      consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                   所付代價之公允值。


      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or        公允值為於計量日期在市場參與者
      paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between            之間進行有序交易就出售資產所收
      market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                取或轉讓負債所支付的價格，不論
      whether that price is directly observable or estimated using              該價格是直接可觀察或使用另一種
      another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value of an           估值方式估計得出。在估計一項資
      asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the                    產或負債之公允值時，本集團考慮
      characteristic of the asset or liability if market participants           市場參與者於計量日期為該資產或
      would take those characteristics into account when pricing the            負債定價時會考慮之特質。就於該
      asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for                等綜合財務報表中計量及╱或披露
      measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated              之公允值乃按此基準釐定，惟於香
      financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for            港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 2 號 以股 份 為 基
      share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of             礎付款 範疇下的以股份為基礎付款
      HKFRS 2 Share-based Payment , leasing transactions that are               之交易、按照香港財務報告準則第
      accounted for in accordance with HKFRS 16 Leases (“HKFRS                  16號租賃（「香港財務報告準則第16
      16”), and measurements that have some similarities to fair                號 」）入 賬 之 租 賃 交 易 及 一 些 類 似 公
      value but are not fair value, such as net realisable value in             允值但非公允值的計量（例如香港會
      HKAS 2 Inventories , or value in use in HKAS 36 Impairment of             計準則第2號存貨 之可變現淨值或香
      Assets .                                                                  港 會 計 準 則 第 36 號資 產 減 值 的 使 用
                                                                                價值）則除外。

     (CONTINUED)

     In addition, for financial reporting purpose, fair value                此外，就財務申報而言，公允值計
     measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the          量根據公允值計量輸入數據之可觀
     degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are           察程度及輸入數據對公允值整體計
     observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value         量之重要性被分類為第1、第2或第3
     measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:            級，載述如下：


     •    Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            •   第1級輸入數據為實體於計量
          markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity            日期在活躍市場就相同資產或
          can access at the measurement date;                                    負債取得之報價（未經調整）；


     •    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                •   第2級輸入數據為就資產或負
          included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset             債直接或間接可觀察之輸入數
          or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                       據，包括在第一級內之報價除
                                                                                 外；及


     •    Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or            •   第3級輸入數據為資產或負債
          liability.                                                             的不可觀察輸入數據。

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                        3.   主要會計政策（續）
      (CONTINUED)

      The principal accounting policies are set out below.                     主要會計政策載列如下：


      Basis of consolidation                                                   綜合賬目基準

      The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial          本綜合財務報表包含本公司以及由
      statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                 本公司及其附屬公司控制的實體之
      Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved where the              財務報表。取得控制權是指本公司：
      Company:


      •     has power over the investee;                                       •    對被投資方行使權力；


      •     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its            •    因參與被投資方之業務而獲得
            involvement with the investee; and                                      或有權獲得可變回報；及


      •     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.            •    有能力行使其權力而影響其回
                                                                                    報。


      The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if           倘事實及情況顯示上述三項控制元
      facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one           素其中一項或多項改變，則本集團
      or more of the three elements of control listed above.                   會重新評估其是否仍然控制被投資
                                                                               方。


      Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains              當本集團取得附屬公司之控制權，
      control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses              便將該附屬公司綜合入賬；當本
      control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses of a        集團失去附屬公司之控制權，便停
      subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are included          止將該附屬公司綜合入賬。具體而
      in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                言，年內收購或出售之附屬公司之
      comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control               收入及支出會於本集團取得控制權
      until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.          當日起計入綜合損益及其他全面收
                                                                               益表，直至本集團對該附屬公司之
                                                                               控制權終止當日為止。


      Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                 損益及其他全面收益的各個項目乃
      income are attributed to the owners of the Company and to                歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
      the non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of             益。即使會導致非控股權益出現虧
      subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and to           絀結餘，附屬公司之總全面收益仍
      the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-           會歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
      controlling interests having a deficit balance.                          益。

     All intragroup assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses            所有集團內成員間有關資產及負
     and cash flows relating to transactions between members of                 債、權益、收入、支出及現金流量
     the Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                         之交易均於綜合賬目時全數對銷。

     proportionate share of net assets of the relevant subsidiaries             持股比例的資產淨值。
     upon liquidation.


     Business combinations or asset acquisitions                                業務合併或資產收購

     Optional concentration test                                                可選集中度測試
     Effective from 1 January 2020, the Group can elect to apply an             自2020年1月1日起，本集團可選擇
     optional concentration test, on a transaction-by-transaction               按逐項交易基準應用可選集中度測
     basis, that permits a simplified assessment of whether an                  試，准許簡化評估一項收購活動及
     acquired set of activities and assets is not a business. The               資產是否並非一項業務。倘所收購
     concentration test is met if substantially all of the fair value of        資產總額的公允值基本上全部集中
     the gross assets acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable         在單個可識別資產或一組類似可識
     asset or group of similar identifiable assets. The gross assets            別資產中，則符合集中度測試。受
     under assessment exclude cash and cash equivalents, deferred               評估的總資產不包括現金及現金等
     tax assets, and goodwill resulting from the effects of deferred            價物、遞延稅項資產、及源自遞延
     tax liabilities. If the concentration test is met, the set of              稅項負債影響之商譽。倘符合集中
     activities and assets is determined not to be a business and no            度測試，該項活動及資產被釐定為
     further assessment is needed.                                              並非業務及毋須作進一步評估。

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                            3.   主要會計政策（續）
      (CONTINUED)

      Business combinations or asset acquisitions                                  業務合併或資產收購
                                                                                           （續）
      (Continued)

      Asset acquisitions                                                           資產收購
      When the Group acquires a group of assets and liabilities that               當本集團收購並不構成業務之一組
      do not constitute a business, the Group identifies and                       資產及負債，本集團假設首先將購
      recognises the individual identifiable assets acquired and                   買價格按各自公允值分配給金融資
      liabilities assumed by allocating the purchase price first to                產╱金融負債，購買價格餘額則按
      financial assets/financial liabilities at the respective fair values,        於購買當日其相關之公允值基準分
      the remaining balance of the purchase price is then allocated                配給其他可識別資產及負債，以識
      to the other identifiable assets and liabilities on the basis of             別及確認所收購之個別可識別資產
      their relative fair values at the date of purchase. Such a                   及負債。這樣的交易成本不會產生
      transaction does not give rise to goodwill or bargain purchase               商譽或討價還價的購買收益。
      gain.


      Investment in a joint venture                                                合營企業權益

      A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties that              合營企業是一種共同安排，據此共
      have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net                 同控制該安排的各方擁有該共同安
      assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the                        排的淨資產。共同控制為一項合約
      contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                   協定共享控制之安排，僅會於決定
      which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities               相關事項須共享控制的各方一致同
      require unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.                    意時方會出現。

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                          3.   主要會計政策（續）
     (CONTINUED)

     Investment in a joint venture (Continued)                                   合營企業權益
                                                                                      （續）

     The results and assets and liabilities of joint venture are                 合營企業之業績及資產與負債乃按
     incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using               權益會計法列入綜合財務報表內。
     the equity method of accounting. The financial statements of                按權益法列賬的合營企業的財務報
     joint ventures used for equity accounting purposes are                      表乃採用本集團在類似情況就類似
     prepared using uniform accounting policies as those of the                  交易及事件所採用的劃一會計政策
     Group for like transactions and events in similar circumstances.            而編製。根據權益法，合營企業權
     Under the equity method, an investment in a joint venture is                益於綜合財務狀況表初始按成本確
     initially recognised in the consolidated statement of financial             認，及其後調整以確認本集團應佔
     position at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the                   該合營企業之損益及其他全面收
     Group’s share of the profit or loss and other comprehensive                 益。當本集團應佔合營企業之虧損
     income of the joint venture. When the Group’s share of losses               超出其於該合營企業之權益（即包括
     of a joint venture exceeds the Group’s interest in that joint               任何實質上構成本集團於該合營企
     venture (which includes any long-term interests that, in                    業之投資淨額之長期權益），則本集
     substance, form part of the Group’s net investment in the joint             團不再確認其應佔之進一步虧損。
     venture), the Group discontinues recognising its share of                   僅會於本集團已產生法定或推定責
     further losses. Additional losses are recognised only to the                任或替該合營企業支付款項之情況
     extent that the Group has incurred legal or constructive                    下，方會確認額外虧損。
     obligations or made payments on behalf of the joint venture.


     An investment in a joint venture is accounted for using the                 合營企業權益於當被投資方成為合
     equity method from the date on which the investee becomes a                 營企業當日採用權益法入賬。收購
     joint venture. On acquisition of the investment in a joint                  合營企業權益時，投資成本超出本
     venture, any excess of the cost of the investment over the                  集團應佔被投資方可識別資產及負
     Group’s share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and          債淨公允值之任何數額乃確認為商
     liabilities of the investee is recognised as goodwill, which is             譽，並包括在該投資的賬面值中。
     included within the carrying amount of the investment. Any                  經重估本集團應佔被投資方可識別
     excess of the Group’s share of the net fair value of the                    資產及負債淨公允值超出投資成本
     identifiable assets and liabilities over the cost of the investment,        之任何數額則立即在收購該投資之
     after reassessment, is recognised immediately in profit or loss in          期間在損益確認。
     the period in which the investment is acquired.

      accordance with HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets as a single                 36 號資 產 減 值 作 為 單 一 資 產 作 減 值
      asset by comparing its recoverable amount (higher of value in            測試，方法為將賬面值與可收回金
      use and fair value less costs of disposal) with its carrying             額（使用價值及公允值之較高者減出
      amount. Any impairment loss recognised is not allocated to any           售成本）加以比較。任何已確認之減

      is recognised in accordance with HKAS 36 to the extent that              分。倘該投資之可收回金額其後增
      the recoverable amount of the investment subsequently                    加 ， 則 根 據 香 港 會 計 準 則 第 36 號 確
      increases.                                                               認該減值虧損之撥回。

      financial statements only to the extent of interests in the joint        業權益，方於本集團之綜合財務報
      venture that are not related to the Group.                               表確認。

     Revenue from contracts with customers                                        來自客戶合約的收入

     The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                      本 集 團 於 完 成 履 約 責 任 時（ 或 就 此 ）
     obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or                 確認收入，即於特定履約責任相關
     services underlying the particular performance obligation is                 的 貨 品 或 服 務 的「 控 制 權 」轉 移 予 客
     transferred to the customer.                                                 戶時。


     A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a                  履約責任指不同的貨品或服務（或一
     bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct        籃子貨品或服務）或大致上相同之一
     goods or services that are substantially the same.                           系列不同的貨品或服務。


     Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over              經參考相關履約責任的完成進度，
     time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction              當達至下面其中一項時，控制權隨
     of the relevant performance obligation if one of the following               時間的推移而轉移及收入隨時間的
     criteria is met:                                                             推移而確認：


     •     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the                  •    客戶同時接收及耗用由本集團
           benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the                         執行本集團履約所提供的效
           Group performs;                                                             益；


     •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset                   •    客戶控制由本集團執行本集團
           that the customer controls as the Group performs; or                        的履約產生及提升的資產；或


     •     the Group’s performance does not create an asset with                  •    本集團的履約不會產生對本集
           an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                        團有其他用途的資產及本集團
           enforceable right to payment for performance completed                      對迄今完成的履約之付款有可
           to date.                                                                    執行之權利。


     Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                 否則，則當客戶取得不同貨品或服
     customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.                    務的控制權時按一個時間點確認收
                                                                                  入。

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3.   主要會計政策（續）

     Leases                                                                  租賃

     Definition of a lease                                                   租賃之定義
     A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the         當合約傳達權利以控制使用一項已
     right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of         識別資產一段期間以換取代價，則
     time in exchange for consideration.                                     該合約乃（或包含）一項租賃。


     For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of          於初始應用日期或之後訂立或修改
     initial application or arising from business combinations, the          或由業務合併引起之合約，本集團
     Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based          根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號 之 定
     on the definition under HKFRS 16 at inception, modification             義於初始時，修改日期及或收購日
     date or acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not        期，倘適用，評估一份合約是否（或
     be reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the contract           包 含 ）一 項 租 賃 。 除 非 合 約 之 條 款
     are subsequently changed. As a practical expedient, leases with         及條件其後改變，此合約將不會再
     similar characteristics are accounted on a portfolio basis when         評估。作為權宜方案，當本集團合
     the Group reasonably expects that the effects on the                    理預期於綜合財務報表之影響將不
     consolidated financial statements would not differ materially           會跟組合內之個別租賃有重大差別
     from individual leases within the portfolio.                            時，類似性質的租賃乃按組合基準
                                                                             入賬。


     The Group as a lessee                                                   本集團作為承租人
     Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                        短期租賃及低值資產租賃
     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption            本 集 團 對 租 約 期 自 開 始 日 期 計 12 個
     to leases of staff quarters and office properties that have a           月或更短者且並無包含購買權之員
     lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement date              工宿舍及辦公室物業應用短期租賃
     and do not contain a purchase option. It also applies the               確認豁免。同時就低值資產租賃也
     recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets. Lease              應用確認豁免。短期租賃及低值資
     payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value assets            產租賃之租賃付款於租約期內按直
     are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis or another           線法或其他系統基準確認為費用。
     systematic basis over the lease term.

      Right-of-use assets                                                     使用權資產
      The cost of right-of-use asset includes:                                使用權資產成本包括：


      •     the amount of the initial measurement of the lease                •   租賃負債初始計量金額；
            liability;


      •     any lease payments made at or before the commencement             •   於開始日期或之前作出之任何
            date, less any lease incentives received; and                         租賃付款，減去任何已收租賃
                                                                                  補助；及


      •     any initial direct costs incurred by the Group.                   •   本集團涉及之任何初始直接成
                                                                                  本。


      Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any accumulated          使用權資產以成本減去任何累計折
      depreciation and impairment losses, and adjusted for any                舊及減值虧損，並就租賃負債任何
      remeasurement of lease liabilities.                                     重新計量作出調整來計量。


      Right-of-use assets in which the Group is reasonably certain to         本集團合理確定於租賃期完結時可
      obtain ownership of the underlying leased assets at the end of          獲得相關租賃資產擁有權之使用權
      the lease term are depreciated from commencement date to                資產，從開始日期至可使用年期完
      the end of the useful life. Otherwise, right-of-use assets are          結計算折舊。否則，使用權資產按
      depreciated on a straight-line basis over the shorter of its            其估計可使用年期及租賃年期較短
      estimated useful life and the lease term.                               者，以直線法計算折舊。


      The Group presents right-of-use assets that do not meet the             本集團將不符合投資物業定義之使
      definition of investment property as a separate line item on the        用權資產於綜合財務狀況表以獨立
      consolidated statement of financial position. The right-of-use          項目列示。符合投資物業定義之使
      assets that meet the definition of investment property are              用權資產則併入「投資物業」列示。
      presented within “investment properties”.

     Lease liabilities                                                           租賃負債
     At the commencement date of a lease, the Group recognises                   於租賃開始日期，本集團以該日未
     and measures the lease liability at the present value of lease              付租賃付款現值確認及計量租賃負
     payments that are unpaid at that date. In calculating the                   債。於計算租賃付款現值時，倘隱

     incremental borrowing rate at the lease commencement date if                本集團則採用租賃開始日期之漸進
     the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily determinable.        借貸率。


     The lease payments include:                                                 租賃付款包括：

            less any lease incentives receivable;                                    款）減去任何應收租賃補助；

            rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at the               租賃付款，按開始日期之指數
            commencement date;                                                       或比率初始計量；


     •      amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                    •   本集團於剩餘價值擔保之預期
            residual value guarantees;                                               應付金額；


     •      the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is              •   倘本集團合理確定將會行使購
            reasonably certain to exercise the option; and                           買權之行使價；及


     •      payments of penalties for terminating a lease, if the lease          •   倘租期影響本集團行使終止租
            term reflects the Group exercising an option to terminate                賃權，終止租賃所支付之罰
            the lease.                                                               金。

      Lease liabilities (Continued)                                          租賃負債（續）
      After the commencement date, lease liabilities are adjusted by         於開始日期後，租賃負債按利息增
      interest accretion and lease payments.                                 長及租賃付款作調整。


      The Group remeasures lease liabilities (and makes a                    每當出現下列情況，本集團重新計
      corresponding adjustment to the related right-of-use assets)           量租賃負債（及對相關使用權資產作
      whenever:                                                              出對應調整）：


      •     the lease term has changed or there is a change in the           •   租期已變更或行使購買權之評
            assessment of exercise of a purchase option, in which                估變更，在此情況下相關租賃
            case the related lease liability is remeasured by                    負債按於重新計量當日之已修
            discounting the revised lease payments using a revised               訂折現率將已修訂租賃付款折
            discount rate at the date of reassessment.                           現來重新計量。


      •     the lease payments change due to changes in market               •   租賃付款變更乃由於按照市
            rental rates following a market rent review/expected                 場租金檢視之市場租金率變
            payment under a guaranteed residual value, in which                  更╱根據保證剩餘價值之預期
            cases the related lease liability is remeasured by                   付款變更，在此情況下相關租
            discounting the revised lease payments using the initial             賃負債按初始折現率將已修訂
            discount rate.                                                       租賃付款折現來重新計量。


      The Group presents lease liabilities as a separate line item on        本集團將租賃負債於綜合財務狀況
      the consolidated statement of financial position.                      表以獨立項目列示。

     Lease modifications                                                     租賃修改
     The Group accounts for a lease modification as a separate lease         本集團將租賃修改入賬為單獨之租
     if:                                                                     賃，倘：


     •     the modification increases the scope of the lease by              •   修改增加租賃範圍，加入一項
           adding the right to use one or more underlying assets;                或多項相關資產之使用權；及
           and


     •     the consideration for the leases increases by an amount           •   租賃代價增加與範圍增加的獨
           commensurate with the stand-alone price for the increase              立價格相稱，並對該獨立價格
           in scope and any appropriate adjustments to that stand-               進行任何適當之調整以反映特
           alone price to reflect the circumstances of the particular            定合同的情況。
           contract.


     For a lease modification that is not accounted for as a separate        就租賃修改並無入賬為獨立租賃而
     lease, the Group remeasures the lease liability based on the            言，本集團按修改租賃之租期以於
     lease term of the modified lease by discounting the revised             修改生效日期之修訂折現率將修訂
     lease payments using a revised discount rate at the effective           租賃付款折現來重新計量租賃負債。
     date of the modification.


     The Group accounts for the remeasurement of lease liabilities           本集團就重新計量租賃負債以相關
     by making corresponding adjustments to the relevant right-of-           使用權資產作出對應調整來入賬。
     use asset. When the modified contract contains a lease                  當修改合約包含租賃部分及一項或
     component and one or more additional lease or non-lease                 多項額外租賃或非租賃部分，本集
     components, the Group allocates the consideration in the                團按租賃部分之相關獨立價格及非
     modified contract to each lease component on the basis of the           租賃部分之合計獨立價格將修改合
     relative stand-alone price of the lease component and the               約之代價分配至各自租賃部分。
     aggregate stand-alone price of the non-lease components.

      The Group as a lessor                                                      集團作為出租人
      Classification and measurement of leases                                   租賃之分類及計量
      Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as finance           集團作為出租人之租賃分類為融資
      or operating leases. Whenever the terms of the lease transfer              或經營租賃。凡租賃之條款規定相
      substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to ownership            關資產擁有權所附帶之一切風險及
      of an underlying asset to the lessee, the contract is classified as        回報實質上轉移至承租人，該租賃
      a finance lease. All other leases are classified as operating              即歸類為融資租賃。其他租賃全部
      leases.                                                                    列作經營租賃。


      Amounts due from lessees under finance leases are recognised               融資租賃承租人應欠金額於開始日
      as receivables at commencement date at amounts equal to net                期以租賃淨投資之相等金額確認為
      investments in the leases, measured using the interest rate                應收賬款，以隱含在相關租賃之利
      implicit in the respective leases. Initial direct costs (other than        率計量。初始直接成本（該等涉及製
      those incurred by manufacturer or dealer lessors) are included             造商或經銷出租人的除外）包含在租
      in the initial measurement of the net investments in the leases.           賃淨投資之初始計量。利息收入分
      Interest income is allocated to accounting periods so as to                配至各會計期間以反映本集團於相
      reflect a constant periodic rate of return on the Group’s net              關租賃淨投資餘額之恆常定期收益
      investment outstanding in respect of the leases.                           率。


      Rental income from operating leases is recognised in profit or             經營租賃產生之租金收入按直線法
      loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant lease.         於相關租賃期內在損益確認。磋商
      Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging an              及安排一項經營租賃涉及的初始直
      operating lease are added to the carrying amount of the leased             接成本乃加至租賃資產之賬面值及
      asset, and such costs are recognised as an expense on a                    該等成本以直線法於租賃期內確認
      straight-line basis over the lease term except for investment              為費用，惟按公允值模式計量之投
      properties measured under fair value model.                                資物業除外。

      Foreign currencies                                                      外幣

      In preparing the financial statements of each individual group          於編製各個別集團實體之財務報表
      entity, transactions in currencies other than the entity’s              時，以該實體功能貨幣以外之貨幣
      functional currency (foreign currencies) are recognised at the          （ 外 幣 ）進 行 之 交 易 均 按 交 易 日 期 之
      rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the transactions.         適用換算滙率確認。於報告期末，
      At the end of the reporting period, monetary items                      以外幣定值之貨幣項目均按結算日
      denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates         之適用滙率重新換算。非貨幣項目
      prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items that are measured           按外幣過往成本計量而毋須重新換
      in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not               算。
      retranslated.


      Exchange differences arising on the settlement of and on the            結算及重新換算貨幣項目所產生之
      retranslation of monetary items are recognised in profit or loss        匯兌差額於其產生期間於損益內確
      in the period in which they arise, except for exchange                  認，惟源自其結算並無計劃及不大
      differences on monetary items receivable from or payable to a           可能出現的應收或應付境外業務之
      foreign operation for which settlement is neither planned nor           貨幣項目的匯兌差額（因此構成境外
      likely to occur (therefore forming part of the net investment in        業務淨投資之一部分）除外，該差額
      the foreign operation), which are recognised initially in other         初始於其他全面收益表確認及當出
      comprehensive income and reclassified from equity to profit or          售或部分出售本集團之權益時重新
      loss on disposal or partial disposal of the Group’s interests.          由權益分類至損益。


      For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial               就呈列綜合財務報表而言，本集團
      statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s foreign           海外經營業務之資產及負債乃採用
      operations are translated into the presentation currency of the         報告期末當日之滙率換算為本集團
      Group (i.e. HK$) using exchange rates prevailing at the end of          之 列 賬 貨 幣（ 即 港 元 ）， 而 收 入 及 支
      each reporting period. Income and expenses items are                    出項目乃按該期間之平均滙率進行
      translated at the average exchange rates for the period, unless         換算，除非滙率於該期間內出現大
      exchange rates fluctuate significantly during the period, in            幅波動則作別論，於此情況下，則
      which case, the exchange rates prevailing at the dates of               採用於交易當日之匯率。所產生之
      transactions are used. Exchange differences arising, if any, are        匯 兌 差 額（ 如 有 ）乃 於 其 他 全 面 收 益
      recognised in other comprehensive income and accumulated in             確認及於滙兌儲備項下累計權益（適
      equity under the heading of translation reserve (attributed to          用時歸入非控股權益）。
      non-controlling interests as appropriate).

     Borrowing costs                                                          借貸成本

     Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,                與購置、建造或生產合資格資產（需
     construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets        一段長時間方可作擬定用途或可供
     that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready          銷售的資產）直接有關之借貸成本均
     for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those           加至該等資產之成本，直至該等資
     assets until such time as the assets are substantially ready for         產大致可作其擬定用途或可供銷售
     their intended use or sale.                                              為止。


     All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the        所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間於
     period in which they are incurred.                                       損益中確認。


     Retirement benefit costs                                                 退休福利成本

     Payments to the Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the “MPF                根 據 強 制 性 公 積 金 計 劃（「 強 積 金 計
     Scheme”) and state-managed retirement benefit scheme are                 劃 」）及 國 家 管 理 的 退 休 福 利 計 劃 的
     recognised as an expense when the employees have rendered                付款於僱員提供服務使其可享有供
     services entitling them to the contributions.                            款時確認為費用。


     Short-term employee benefits                                             短期僱員福利

     Short-term employee benefits are recognised at the                       短期僱員福利按當僱員提供服務時
     undiscounted amount of the benefits expected to be paid as               預期將支付之未折現福利金額確
     and when employees rendered services. All short-term                     認。所有短期僱員福利確認為費
     employee benefits are recognised as an expense unless another            用，除非另一香港財務報告準則規
     HKFRS requires or permits the inclusion of the benefit in the            定或准許將福利概括入一項資產的
     cost of an asset.                                                        成本中。

      Taxation                                                                  稅項

      Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                利得稅支出指現時應付稅項及遞延
      payable and deferred tax.                                                 稅項的總和。


      The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.        現時應付稅項乃按年度應課稅盈利
      Taxable profit differs from ‘profit before tax’ because of items          計算。應課稅盈利與除稅前盈利不
      of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other              同乃源於其他年度應課稅或可扣稅
      years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                 的收入或開支項目及從未課稅及扣
      Group’s current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been          稅的項目。本集團之本期稅項乃按
      enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting              報告期末已實行或大致上已實行之
      period.                                                                   稅率計算。


      Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between               遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表之資
      the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated        產及負債賬面值與計算應課稅盈
      financial statements and the corresponding tax base used in               利所採用相應稅基的暫時差額而確
      the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are           認。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課
      generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences.               稅暫時差額予以確認。遞延稅項資
      Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible           產一般就所有可扣稅暫時差額確
      temporary differences to the extent that it is probable that              認，惟僅於可能有應課稅盈利將可
      taxable profits will be available against which those deductible          以使用該可扣稅暫時差額時確認。
      temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred assets and           倘暫時差額乃由初始確認一項不影
      liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises         響應課稅盈利或會計盈利的交易的
      from the initial recognition of assets and liabilities in a               其他資產及負債所引起，則有關遞
      transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the               延資產及負債不予確認。
      accounting profit.


      Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary             遞延稅項負債一般就與附屬公司之
      differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               投資及合營企業權益而引致之應課
      interest in joint venture, except where the Group is able to              稅暫時差額而確認，惟若本集團可
      control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is                控制暫時差額之撥回及暫時差額有
      probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in the            可能不會於可見將來撥回之情況則
      foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible           除外。與該等投資及權益相關的可
      temporary differences associated with such investments and                扣稅暫時差額所產生的遞延稅項資
      interest are only recognised to the extent that it is probable            產，僅在可能有足夠應課稅盈利可
      that there will be sufficient taxable profits against which to            以使用暫時差額之效益且預計於可
      utilise the benefits of the temporary differences and they are            見將來可以撥回時確認。
      expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.

     Taxation (Continued)                                                        稅項
                                                                                  （續）

     The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the               遞延稅項資產之賬面值於各報告期
     end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is            末作檢討，並在沒可能會有足夠應
     no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be                  課稅盈利以收回全部或部分資產時
     available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.                加以遞減。


     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates           遞延稅項資產及負債按預期負債清
     that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is          償或資產變現期間內應用之稅率，
     settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and tax laws)          以報告期末已生效或實際上已生效
     that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of               之稅率（及稅法）為基準計量。
     the reporting period.


     The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects             遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映本
     the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                   集團於報告期末所預期對收回或償
     which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period, to             還其資產及負債之賬面值之方式所
     recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and liabilities.        產生的稅務後果。


     For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing                      就本集團確認使用權資產及相關租
     transactions in which the Group recognises the right-of-use                 賃負債之租賃交易計量遞延稅項而
     assets and the related lease liabilities, the Group first                   言，本集團首先釐定稅項扣減是否
     determines whether the tax deductions are attributable to the               歸因於使用權資產或租賃負債。
     right-of-use assets or the lease liabilities.

      estimate accounted for on a prospective basis.                             基準入賬。

     any directly attributable expenditure. Subsequent to initial            括任何直接應佔支出。在初始確認

     lives and after taking into account of their estimated residual         可使用年期內撇銷其成本。
     value, using the straight-line method.


     An investment property is derecognised upon disposal or when            當投資物業出售或永久停止使用及
     the investment property is permanently withdrawn from use               預計不會從該物業出售中獲得未
     and no future economic benefits are expected from its                   來經濟利益時，則剔除確認投資物
     disposals. Any gain or loss arising on derecognition of the             業。剔除確認物業產生之收益或虧
     property (calculated as the difference between the net disposal         損（按其出售所得款項淨額與該資產
     proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset) is included in           賬面值間之差額計算）於剔除確認該
     the profit or loss in the period in which the property is               物業之期間計入損益。
     derecognised.

      amount of the relevant asset is estimated in order to determine          相關資產的可收回金額，以釐定減
      the extent of the impairment loss, if any.                               值虧損的幅度（如有）。

      Group estimates the recoverable amount of the cash-                      計其資產所屬現金產生單位的可收
      generating unit to which the asset belongs.                              回金額。

      assets are allocated to the relevant cash-generating unit when           可以建立合理且一致的分配基礎
      a reasonable and consistent basis of allocation can be                   時，企業資產乃分配予相關現金產

      group of cash generating units for which a reasonable and                合理和一致的最小現金產生單元
      consistent allocation basis can be established. The recoverable          組。企業資產所屬現金產生單位或
      amount is determined for the cash-generating unit or group of            現金產生單元組被釐定可回收金

     prior years. A reversal of an impairment loss is recognised              回之減值虧損即時在損益中確認。

      necessary to make the sales.                                               及銷售所需之成本。

      the instruments. All regular way purchases or sales of financial           所有常規方式買賣金融資產按貿易

      Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or sales of                   式買賣指要求按市場之法規或慣例

      place.

     (other than financial assets and financial liabilities at fair value        值計入損益之金融資產及金融負債

     attributable to the acquisition of financial assets or financial            因收購按公允值計入損益之金融資

     immediately in profit or loss.                                              本即時於損益確認。


     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                實際利率法是一種計算金融資產及

     relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that               際利率是以精確將金融資產或金融
     exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments               負 債 預 測 可 使 用 年 期 或（ 如 適 用 ）更
     (including all fees and points paid or received that form an                短期間估計未來現金收入或付款（包
     integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and         括所有已付或已收並構成實際利率
     other premiums or discounts) through the expected life of the               整體之費用及點子、交易成本及其
     financial asset or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a            他溢價或折讓）折現至初始確認時之
     shorter period, to the net carrying amount on initial                       賬面淨值之比率。
     recognition.

      Classification and subsequent measurement of financial assets           金融資產的分類及其後計量
      Financial assets that meet the following conditions are                 金融資產當符合至以下情況時則其
      subsequently measured at amortised cost:                                後以攤銷成本計量：


      •     the financial asset is held within a business model whose         •   金融資產由以收取合約現金流
            objective is to collect contractual cash flows; and                   量為目的之業務模式持有；及


      •     the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to cash        •   合約條款導致於特定日期僅按
            flows that are solely payments of principal and interest              未清償本金金額支付本金及利
            on the principal amount outstanding.                                  息之現金流量。


      Financial assets that meet the following conditions are                 金融資產當符合至以下情況時則其
      subsequently measured at fair value through other                       後 按 公 允 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收 益（「 公
      comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”):                                        允值計入其他全面收益」）計量：


      •     the financial asset is held within a business model whose         •   金融資產由同時以收取合約現
            objective is achieved by both collecting contractual cash             金流量及出售為目的之業務模
            flows and selling; and                                                式持有；及


      •     the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to cash        •   合約條款導致於特定日期僅按
            flows that are solely payments of principal and interest              未清償本金金額支付本金及利
            on the principal amount outstanding.                                  息之現金流量。


      All other financial assets are subsequently measured at fair            所有其他金融資產其後按公允值
      value through profit or loss (“FVTPL”), except that initial             計 入 損 益（「 按 公 允 值 計 入 損 益 」）
      recognition of a financial asset the Group may irrevocably elect        計量，惟於初始確認一項金融資產
      to present subsequent changes in fair value of an equity                時，本集團可能不可撤銷地選擇於
      investment in other comprehensive income if that equity                 其他全面收益呈列權益投資公允值
      investment is neither held for trading nor contingent                   其後變動（倘該權益投資既非持有作
      consideration recognised by an acquirer in a business                   買賣亦非於應用香港財務準則第3號
      combination to which HKFRS 3 Business Combinations applies.             業務合併 時收購者於業務合併確認
                                                                              之或然代價）則除外。

           hedging instrument.                                                         之衍生工具。

      method for financial assets measured subsequently at                     金融資產及其後按公允值計入其他
      amortised cost and debt instruments/receivables subsequently             全面收益計量之債務工具╱應收款
      measured at FVTOCI. Interest income is calculated by applying            項以實際利率法確認。利息收入對
      the effective interest rate to the gross carrying amount of a            金融資產賬面總值應用實際利率法
      financial asset, except for financial assets that have                   計算，除非該金融資產其後轉變為
      subsequently become credit-impaired (see below). For financial           信 貸 減 值（ 見 下 文 ）。 就 金 融 資 產 其
      assets that have subsequently become credit-impaired, interest           後轉變為信貸減值，利息收入按該
      income is recognised by applying the effective interest rate to          金融資產下一報告期間之攤銷成本
      the amortised cost of the financial asset from the next reporting        應用實際利率法確認。若信貸減值
      period. If the credit risk on the credit-impaired financial              的金融工具之信貸風險改善而該金
      instrument improves so that the financial asset is no longer             融資產再毋須信貸減值，利息收入
      credit-impaired, interest income is recognised by applying the           對金融資產於自釐定該資產再毋須
      effective interest rate to the gross carrying amount of the              信貸減值之報告期間開始對金融資
      financial asset from the beginning of the reporting period               產賬面總值採用實際利率法確認。
      following the determination that the asset is no longer credit
      impaired.

     Impairment of financial assets                                            金融資產減值
     The Group recognises a loss allowance for ECL on financial                本集團於金融資產就預期信貸虧損
     assets which are subject to impairment assessment under                   確認虧損撥備乃視乎香港財務準則
     HKFRS 9 (including trade and other receivables, loan to a joint           第 9 號 項 下 的 減 值 評 估（ 包 括 應 收 貿
     venture, bills receivables discounted with recourse, amounts              易及其他賬款、合營企業貸款、附
     due from related companies and bank balances.) The amount                 追索權應收票據貼現、應收關連公
     of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect changes in            司款項及銀行結餘）。預期信貸虧損
     credit risk since initial recognition.                                    金額於每個報告日期更新以反映自
                                                                               初始確認以來之信貸風險變動。


     Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible        終身預期信貸虧損指由所有可能違
     default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument.         約事件超出相關工具預期壽命將引
     In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the portion              致 之 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 。 相 反 ，12 個 月
     of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events            預 期 信 貸 虧 損（「 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧
     that are possible within 12 months after the reporting date.              損 」）指 終 身 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 預 期 從 可
     Assessment are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss           能 於 報 告 日 期 後 12 個 月 內 的 違 約 事
     experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,        件引致之部分。所作評估基於本集
     general economic conditions and an assessment of both the                 團過往信貸虧損記錄，並調整債務
     current conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast          人的特定因素，普遍經濟狀況及同
     of future conditions.                                                     時對於報告日期之現況及預測未來
                                                                               狀況之評估。

      The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade receivables.            本集團通常就應收貿易賬款確認終
                                                                                 身預期信貸虧損。


      For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                     就所有其他工具，本集團以相等於
      allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been a                   12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 計 量 虧 損 撥
      significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, in          備，除非自初始確認後信貸風險顯
      which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The assessment               著增加，本集團則確認終身預期信
      of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on                   貸虧損。評估是否應確認終身預期
      significant increases in the likelihood or risk of a default               信貸虧損乃基於自初始確認後發生
      occurring since initial recognition.                                       違約的可能性或風險是否顯著增加。


      (i)     Significant increase in credit risk                                (i)   信貸風險顯著增加
              In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                       於評估自初始確認後信貸風險
              significantly since initial recognition, the Group compares              是否顯著增加時，本集團將於
              the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument              報告日期金融工具發生違約
              as at the reporting date with the risk of a default                      之風險與於初始確認日期金融
              occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of                  工具發生違約之風險作比較。
              initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group                作出評估時，本集團同時考慮
              considers both quantitative and qualitative information                  合理及有理據的定量及定性資
              that is reasonable and supportable, including historical                 料，包括過往經驗及無需花費
              experience and forward-looking information that is                       成本或精力而可得之前瞻性資
              available without undue cost or effort.                                  料。

              Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment, the                     無論上述評估之結果如何，當
              Group presumes that the credit risk has increased                            合 約 付 款 逾 期 超 過 30 天 ， 本
              significantly since initial recognition when contractual                     集團即假定信貸風險自初始確
              payments are more than 30 days past due, unless the                          認以來顯著增加，除非本集團
              Group has reasonable and supportable information that                        有合理及有理據的資料另作證
              demonstrates otherwise.                                                      明。


              Despite the aforegoing, the Group assumes that the                           儘管如此，若債務工具於報告
              credit risk on a debt instrument has not increased                           日期被釐定為低信貸風險，本
              significantly since initial recognition if the debt instrument               集團則假設該債務工具之信貸
              is determined to have low credit risk at the reporting                       風險自初始確認以來並無顯著
              date. A debt instrument is determined to have low credit                     增加。債務工具被釐定為低信
              risk if i) it has a low risk of default, ii) the borrower has a              貸風險，倘i)其違約風險屬低；
              strong capacity to meet its contractual cash flow                            ii) 借 款 人 具 強 勁 能 力 於 近 期 履
              obligations in the near term and iii) adverse changes in                     行其合約現金流量責任；及iii)
              economic and business conditions in the longer term                          長遠的經濟及業務狀況不利逆
              may, but will not necessarily, reduce the ability of the                     轉可能，但不一定，減低借款
              borrower to fulfil its contractual cash flow obligations.                    人履行其合約現金流量責任的
              The Group considers a debt instrument to have low credit                     能力。當債務工具於內部或外
              risk when it has an internal or external credit rating of                    部信貸評級為按全球理解定義
              ‘investment grade’ as per globally understood definitions.                   的「投資級別」，本集團認為該
                                                                                           債務工具屬低風險。


              The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the                        本集團定期監控用作識別是否
              criteria used to identify whether there has been a                           出現信貸風險顯著增加的標準
              significant increase in credit risk and revises them as                      之有效性及當適用時修訂標準
              appropriate to ensure that the criteria are capable of                       以確保該標準乃於金額逾期前

              amount becomes past due.

     (ii)   Definition of default                                              (ii)   違約的定義
            For internal credit risk management, the Group considers                  就內部信貸風險管理，當內部
            an event of default occurs when information developed                     開發資料或取得外部資料顯示
            internally or obtained from external sources indicates that               債務人不太可能全數支付其債
            the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the                權 人 ， 包 括 本 集 團（ 並 未 考 慮
            Group, in full (without taking into account any collaterals               本 集 團 持 有 任 何 抵 押 品 ）， 本
            held by the Group).                                                       集團認為發生違約事件。

            lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                            違約標準更為合適。

      (iii)     Credit-impaired financial assets                                    (iii)   信貸減值金融資產
                A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                       當一項及多項違約事件發生令
                events of default that have a detrimental impact on the                     該金融資產估計未來現金流量
                estimated future cash flows of that financial asset have                    具不利影響，金融資產則信
                occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired                貸減值。金融資產信貸減值的
                includes observable data about the following events:                        證據包括以下事件之可觀察數
                                                                                            據：


                (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                 (a)   發行人或借款人存在嚴重
                      borrower;                                                                   財政困難；


                (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past due                   (b)   違約，即如違約或過期事
                      event;                                                                      件；


                (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                        (c)   借款人之貸款人，就有關
                      contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                              借款人財政困難之經濟或
                      financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower                        合約原因，給予借款人一
                      a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not                                般貸款人不會考慮的讓
                      otherwise consider;                                                         步；

      (v)     Measurement and recognition of ECL                                  (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
              The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                   預期信貸虧損計量具違約可能
              of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the                 性 ， 默 認 虧 損（ 即 違 約 的 損 失
              loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The              幅 度 ）及 違 約 曝 光 的 功 能 。 違
              assessment of the probability of default and loss given                   約可能性及默認虧損的評估乃
              default is based on historical data and forward-looking                   基於過往數據經前瞻性資料作
              information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                   調整。預期信貸虧損的估計反
              probability-weighted amount that is determined with the                   映不偏倚及概率加權金額乃按
              respective risks of default occurring as the weights.                     發生違約相關風險的比重來釐
                                                                                        定。


              Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                          一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根
              contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                       據合約所有應欠本集團合約現
              accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the                  金流量與本集團於初始確認時
              Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                     預期收到，按實際利率折現的
              interest rate determined at initial recognition.                          現金流量的差額。

     the Group neither transfers nor retains substantially all the risks        方會剔除確認金融資產。倘本集團

     transferred asset, the Group recognises its retained interest in           回報及繼續控制已轉移資產，本集

     pay. If the Group retains substantially all the risks and rewards          須支付金額確認聯營負債。倘本集
     of ownership of a transferred financial asset, the Group                   團保留已轉移金融資產之絕大部分
     continues to recognise the financial asset and also recognises a           風險及回報，本集團繼續確認該金
     collateralised borrowing for the proceeds received.                        融資產並就已收取款項確認抵押借
                                                                                貸。

      Financial liabilities and equity                                         金融負債及權益工具
      Classification as debt or equity                                         分類為債務或權益
      Debt and equity instruments are classified as either financial           債務及權益工具按所訂立之合約安
      liabilities or as equity in accordance with the substance of the         排性質，以及金融負債及權益工具
      contractual arrangements and the definitions of a financial              之定義而分類為金融負債或權益。
      liability and an equity instrument.


      Equity instruments                                                       權益工具
      An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a residual           權益工具為證明一實體經扣除其所
      interest in the assets of an entity after deducting all of its           有負債後的資產剩餘權益之任何合
      liabilities. Equity instruments issued by the Company are                約。本公司發行的權益工具按收取
      recognised at the proceeds received, net of direct issue costs.          所得款項減直接發行成本確認。


      Perpetual instruments, which include no contractual obligation           永久性工具，其中包括本集團無合
      for the Group to deliver cash or other financial assets or the           約義務交付現金或其他金融資產或
      Group has the sole discretion to defer payment of distribution           本集團可全權酌情決定延期支付分
      and redemption of principal amount indefinitely are classified as        派及無限期贖回本金，被分類為權
      equity instruments.                                                      益工具。


      Repurchase of the Company’s own equity instruments is                    購回本公司自身權益工具直接於權
      recognised and deducted directly in equity. No gain or loss is           益中扣減及確認。購買、出售、發
      recognised in profit or loss on the purchase, sale, issue or             行及註銷本公司自身權益工具概不
      cancellation of the Company’s own equity instruments.                    會在損益確認盈虧。


      Financial liabilities                                                    金融負債
      All financial liabilities are subsequently measured at amortised         所有金融負債是其後以實際利率法
      cost using the effective interest method or at FVTPL.                    按攤銷成本或按公允值計入損益計
                                                                               量。

           guarantee contract or a designated and effective hedging                   具為財務擔保合同或指定及有
           instrument.                                                                效對沖工具。
