     For financial liabilities that are designated as at FVTPL, the              就指定為按公允值計入損益之金融
     amount of change in the fair value of the financial liability that          負債而言，金融負債公允值變動金
     is attributable to changes in the credit risk of that liability is          額乃該金融負債應佔信貸風險之變
     recognised in other comprehensive income, unless the                        動，於其他全面收益確認，除非於
     recognition of the effects of changes in the liability’s credit risk        其他全面收益確認負債之信貸風險
     in other comprehensive income would create or enlarge an                    變動影響將產生或擴大於損益賬的
     accounting mismatch in profit or loss. Changes in fair value                一項會計錯配。於其他全面收益確
     attributable to a financial liability’s credit risk that are                認之金融負債信貸風險之應佔公允
     recognised in other comprehensive income are not                            值變動，其後不會重新分類至損
     subsequently reclassified to profit or loss; instead, they are              益；取而代之，於剔除確認金融負
     transferred to retained profits upon derecognition of the                   債時將轉入留存盈利中。
     financial liability.


     Financial liabilities at amortised cost                                     按攤銷成本之金融負債
     Financial liabilities including bank borrowings, amounts due to             金融負債包括銀行借貸、應付關連
     related companies, trade and other payables and advances                    公司款項、應付貿易及其他賬款及
     drawn on bills receivables discounted with recourse are                     附追索權之應收票據貼現預付款，
     subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the effective                乃隨後以實際利率法按攤銷成本計
     interest method.                                                            量。


     Derecognition of financial liabilities                                      剔除確認金融負債
     The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only                 僅於本集團之責任獲解除、註銷或
     when, the Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or                  屆滿時，本集團方會剔除確認金融
     have expired. The difference between the carrying amount of                 負債。剔除確認之金融負債賬面值
     the financial liability derecognised and the consideration paid             與已付及應付代價之差額於損益中
     and payable is recognised in profit or loss.                                確認。


     Offsetting a financial asset and a financial liability                      金融資產與金融負債之對銷
     A financial asset and a financial liability are offset and the net          當及僅當本集團有法定行使權力去
     amount presented in the consolidated statement of financial                 對銷已確認金額，並擬以淨額結
     position when, and only when, the Group currently has a                     算，或同時變現資產及清償負債
     legally enforceable right to set off the recognised amounts; and            時，金融資產及金融負債方予對銷
     intends either to settle on a net basis, or to realise the asset            並以淨額於綜合財務狀況表列示。
     and settle the liability simultaneously.

      differ from these estimates.

      ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised          閱。如對會計估計之修訂僅影響進

      affects only that period, or in the period of the revision and           訂，或如修訂同時影響目前及未來
      future periods if the revision affects both current and future           期間，則於修訂及未來期間確認。
      periods.

      receivables. In calculating the ECL, the loss rates are estimated        式計算預期信貸虧損。於計算預期

      credit-rating agencies and adjusted for forward-looking                  似 虧 損 模 式 及 追 償 率（ 國 際 信 用 評
      information that is available without undue cost or effort. Such         級 機 構 所 引 用 ）之 不 同 債 務 人 進 行
      calculation of ECL has involved subjective judgment and                  分組，並就無需花費成本或精力便
      management estimates. At every reporting date, the loss rates            可得之前膽性資料作調整而得出。
      are reassessed and changes in the forward-looking information            就此計算預期信貸虧損涉及主觀判
      are considered. In addition, credit-impaired trade receivables           斷及管理層的估計。於每一報告日
      are assessed for ECL individually.                                       期，會再次評估虧損率及考慮前瞻

                                                                                             （ 第 7 號 ）條 例
     which introduces the two-tiered profits tax rates regime. The         草 案（「 條 例 草 案 」）， 條 例 草 案 引
     Bill was signed into law on 28 March 2018 and was gazetted            入利得稅兩級制稅率。該條例草案
     on the following day. Under the two-tiered profits tax rates          於 2018 年 3 月 28 日 簽 署 成 為 法 律 ，
     regime, the first HK$2 million of profits of the qualifying group     並於翌日在憲報刊登。根據兩級制
     entity will be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2 million         利得稅率，合資格集團實體的首兩
     will be taxed at 16.5%. The profits of group entities not             百 萬 港 元 盈 利 將 按 8.25% 徵 稅 ， 超
     qualifying for the two-tiered profits tax rates regime will           過 兩 百 萬 港 元 的 盈 利 將 按 16.5% 徵
     continue to be taxed at a flat rate of 16.5%.                         稅。不符合利得稅兩級制資格的集
                                                                           團 實 體 之 盈 利 將 繼 續 按 16.5% 的 劃
                                                                           一稅率徵稅。

19. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                    19. 投資物業

     The Group leases out commercial and industrial properties                 本集團根據經營租賃按每月付租出
     under operating leases with rentals payable monthly. The leases           租商業及工業物業。租賃通常初始
     typically run for an initial period of 1 to 10 years, with unilateral     年期為1至10年，承租人單方面有權
     rights to extend the lease beyond initial period held by lessees          延續租賃至初始年期外。大部分租
     only. Majority of the lease contracts contain annual lease                賃合約在租期內固定年度租賃付款。
     payment that are fixed over the lease term.


     The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of          本集團在租賃安排並無承受外幣風
     the lease arrangements, as all leases are denominated in the              險，因所有租賃乃按集團實體相關
     respective functional currencies of group entities. The lease             功能貨幣計值。租賃合約並無包含
     contracts do not contain residual value guarantee and lessee’s            剩餘價值保證及承租人於租期完結
     option to purchase the property at the end of lease term.                 時購入物業之權利。

36. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               36. 資本風險管理
     The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the           本集團管理其資本以確保本集團的
     Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                實體可持續經營業務，並透過最佳
     maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation         債務及權益比例為權益持有人提供
     of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy           最高回報。本集團的整體政策維持
     remains unchanged from prior year.                                     與去年一致。

     The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which            本集團的資本結構包括債務，當中
     include the bank borrowings and lease liabilities as disclosed in      包括分別於附註31及29披露之銀行
     note 31 and 29, respectively, and perpetual capital security and       借貸及租賃負債，以及永續資本證
     equity attributable to equity holders of the Company,                  券及本公司權益持有人應佔權益（包
     comprising issued share capital and various reserves.                  括已發行股本及多項儲備）。

     The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a         本公司董事不時檢討資本結構。作
     regular basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the      為檢討的一部分，董事考慮資本成
     cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of            本以及與各類資本有關的風險。根
     capital. Based on recommendations of the directors, the Group          據董事的建議，本集團將通過派付
     will balance its overall capital structure through the payment of      股息、發行新股及購回股份以及發
     dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as well as the         行新債或贖回現債平衡其整體資本
     issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                  結構。

          Liquidity risk                                                      流動資金風險
          In management of the liquidity risk, the Group monitors             就管理流動資金風險而言，本
          and maintains a level of cash and cash equivalents                  集團監察及保持其視為足以為
          deemed adequate by management to finance the                        本集團營運提供資金，以及減
